Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any biological module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 190. The projective resolution has length at most 295 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_190(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 190. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^295(S, F) is trivial. QED.
